                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  bottom">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Impressive Power</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Prius features a powerful hybrid engine: Underneath the hood is a fifth-generation Toyota Hybrid System with 196 net combined horsepower on AWD models, resulting in an impressive 0-60 mph of 7.0 seconds <sup tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="disclaimer" data-disclaimer="performance_fig"></sup> for AWD models.</p>
                      </div>

                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  right">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Dynamic Handling</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">With a low center of gravity, Prius is designed to perform. Feel its nimble handling in every turn and stability during acceleration and braking, for a drive that’s all-around confident.</p>

                    <div class="feature-tile-content vertical" data-aa-content-section="Performance" data-aa-content-subsection="Drive Modes" data-aa-component="TO Feature tile" data-aa-component-id="to_feature_tile_1523262984" data-aa-component-path="/content/tcom/us/en/prius/2027/jcr:content/root/responsivegrid/tabbed_container_v2_/item_1679516395508_c/tcom-section/to_feature_tiles_wra/tofeaturetile/to_feature_tile_1523262984" data-aa-num-children="4" data-aa-index="3" style="--backgroundColor: #D7D4D4">
                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  top">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Drive Modes</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">EV Mode <sup tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="disclaimer" data-disclaimer="ev_mode"></sup> runs solely on battery power at low speeds for short distances. Power Mode sharpens acceleration; ECO Mode helps maximize fuel efficiency; and Normal Mode blends fuel economy with performance.</p>
                      </div>
                     </div>
                    </div>

                   <div class="to-feature-tile">
                    <div class="feature-tile-content vertical" data-aa-content-section="Performance" data-aa-content-subsection="Impressive Acceleration" data-aa-component="TO Feature tile" data-aa-component-id="to_feature_tile_268102017" data-aa-component-path="/content/tcom/us/en/prius/2027/jcr:content/root/responsivegrid/tabbed_container_v2_/item_1679516395508_c/tcom-section/to_feature_tiles_wra/tofeaturetile/to_feature_tile_268102017" data-aa-num-children="4" data-aa-index="4" style="--backgroundColor: #D7D4D4">
                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  top">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Impressive Acceleration</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Prius delivers power that goes from 0-60 in 7.2 seconds with Front-Wheel Drive (FWD) and 7.0 seconds with All-Wheel Drive (AWD). <sup tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="disclaimer" data-disclaimer="performance_fig"></sup></p>

                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Prius’ Hybrid Synergy Drive® system continues with great efficiency. With a 57 EPA-estimated combined MPG rating on the LE trim, <sup tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="disclaimer" data-disclaimer="mpg_2027_prius"></sup> Prius encourages you to go farther than you thought possible.</p>

                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">60/40 Split Fold-Down Rear Seats</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Fold down either side of Prius' 60/40 rear seats to adapt to what you’ve got. Fold them both down to maximize space, and you'll be surprised at how much you can bring along for the ride.</p>

                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  top">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Ample Cargo Space</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Open Prius' rear hatch to reveal ample cargo room for your things. With 23.8 cu. ft. of cargo capacity on the LE trim with seats up, there’s room to make the most of your adventures.</p>
                      </div>

                   <div class="to-feature-tile">
                    <div class="feature-tile-content vertical" data-aa-content-section="Capability" data-aa-content-subsection="Electronic On-Demand All-Wheel Drive" data-aa-component="TO Feature tile" data-aa-component-id="to_feature_tile_268102017" data-aa-component-path="/content/tcom/us/en/prius/2027/jcr:content/root/responsivegrid/tabbed_container_v2_/item_1748889303734/tcom-section/to_feature_tiles_wra/tofeaturetile/to_feature_tile_268102017" data-aa-num-children="4" data-aa-index="4" style="--backgroundColor: #D7D4D4">
                     <div class="content-container pixels-56  top">
                      <div class="header-content">
                       <h3 class="heading-04 heading">Electronic On-Demand All-Wheel Drive</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div class="copy-container">
                       <p class="body-03 description">Get peace of mind in less than perfect weather. Dedicated electric drive motors are designed to automatically provide power to the rear wheels when Prius’ available Electronic On-Demand All-Wheel Drive (AWD) senses slipping. When extra traction isn’t required, power will continue to be sent to the front wheels.</p>
                      </div>
                     </div>

                       <div class="title body-03 font-semi-bold">
                        Hybrid Powertrain
                       </div>
                       <div class="description body-03">
                        Hybrid Electric Vehicles run on a combination of gas and battery-powered electric motor to maximize power and efficiency. They start, refuel and handle just like the gas-only Toyotas you already know and love.

                      </div>
                      <div class="slide-panel bottom">
                       <div class="title body-03 font-semi-bold">
                        Plug-in Hybrid Powertrain
                       </div>
                       <div class="description body-03">
                        Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicles run just like hybrids, using a combination of fuel and battery powered electric motor, but have a larger battery pack so can run farther on electric power alone. When the battery uses up its charge, it runs like a regular hybrid.
                       </div><a href="/priuspluginhybrid/features/mpg_other_price/1235/1237/1239" title="Plug-in Hybrid Powertrain" tabindex="-1" data-aa-action="aa-link" data-link-metrics="aa-link" data-aa-link_button_action="Learn More link" data-aa-link_type_title="Plug-in Hybrid Powertrain" data-primary-href="/priuspluginhybrid/features/mpg_other_price/1235/1237/1239" class="slide-cta vis-link black no-image  "> <span class="link-text" aria-hidden="true">Learn More

           <div class="description ">
            <p>Beyond Zero is Toyota’s vision to reach carbon neutrality with our products, services and operations, and go beyond, as we find new ways to make a positive impact on our planet and society. The Hybrid EV Prius is one of the many ways we are reducing carbon emissions to drive toward our Beyond Zero vision.</p>

          <div class="custom-code aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
           <script type="module" src="https://prod-widgets-tabbed.saas.zappyride.com/tabbedWidget.js">
</script>
           <script>
  (function () {
    var PENDING_TAG = "zr-tabbed-widget-pending";
    var FINAL_TAG = "zr-tabbed-widget";
    var MODAL_CONTENT_SELECTOR = ".incentives-xf-modal .modal-content";
    var TRIGGER_SELECTOR = ".incentives-xf-modal-button";
    var WATCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000;
    var WATCH_INTERVAL_MS = 120;
    var isPolling = false;

    function getCookie(name) {
      var match = document.cookie.match(
        new RegExp(
          "(?:^|;\\s*)" +
            name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&") +
            "=([^;]*)",
        ),
      );
      return match ? decodeURIComponent(match[1]) : null;
    }

    function patchPostcodeNode(node, zipcode) {
      if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) {
        return false;
      }

      if (!zipcode) {
        return false;
      }

      var upgraded = document.createElement(FINAL_TAG);

      for (var i = 0; i < node.attributes.length; i++) {
        var attr = node.attributes[i];
        if (attr.name !== "postcode") {
          upgraded.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value);
        }
      }

      upgraded.setAttribute("postcode", zipcode);

      while (node.firstChild) {
        upgraded.appendChild(node.firstChild);
      }

      if (node.parentNode) {
        node.parentNode.replaceChild(upgraded, node);
        return true;
      }

      return false;
    }

    async function patchPostcodeLocalStorage(zipcode) {
      if (!zipcode) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        var stored = localStorage.getItem(FINAL_TAG);

        if (!stored) {
          return;
        }

        var urlDecoded = decodeURIComponent(stored);
        var binaryString = atob(urlDecoded);
        var bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
        for (var i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
          bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
        }

        var ds = new DecompressionStream("deflate");
        var dsWriter = ds.writable.getWriter();
        dsWriter.write(bytes);
        dsWriter.close();

        var chunks = [];
        var dsReader = ds.readable.getReader();
        while (true) {
          var chunk = await dsReader.read();
          if (chunk.done) {
            break;
          }
          chunks.push(chunk.value);
        }

        var totalLength = chunks.reduce(function (acc, c) { return acc + c.length; }, 0);
        var combined = new Uint8Array(totalLength);
        var offset = 0;
        for (var j = 0; j < chunks.length; j++) {
          combined.set(chunks[j], offset);
          offset += chunks[j].length;
        }

        var data = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(combined));
        data.postcode = zipcode;

        var cs = new CompressionStream("deflate");
        var csWriter = cs.writable.getWriter();
        csWriter.write(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data)));
        csWriter.close();

        var compChunks = [];
        var csReader = cs.readable.getReader();
        while (true) {
          var compChunk = await csReader.read();
          if (compChunk.done) {
            break;
          }
          compChunks.push(compChunk.value);
        }

        var compLength = compChunks.reduce(function (acc, c) { return acc + c.length; }, 0);
        var compBytes = new Uint8Array(compLength);
        var compOffset = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < compChunks.length; k++) {
          compBytes.set(compChunks[k], compOffset);
          compOffset += compChunks[k].length;
        }

        var binaryStr = "";
        for (var l = 0; l < compBytes.length; l++) {
          binaryStr += String.fromCharCode(compBytes[l]);
        }

        localStorage.setItem(FINAL_TAG, encodeURIComponent(btoa(binaryStr)));
      } catch (e) {
        // If decode/encode fails, leave localStorage untouched
      }
    }

    async function patchPostcode(container) {
      var zipcode = getCookie("zipcode");

      if (!zipcode) {
        return 0;
      }

      if (!container || container.nodeType !== 1) {
        return 0;
      }

      await patchPostcodeLocalStorage(zipcode);

      var upgradedCount = 0;
      var nodes = container.querySelectorAll(PENDING_TAG);

      for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        if (patchPostcodeNode(nodes[i], zipcode)) {
          upgradedCount++;
        }
      }

      return upgradedCount;
    }

    function getModalContent() {
      return document.querySelector(MODAL_CONTENT_SELECTOR);
    }

    function tryUpgradeModalWidgets() {
      var modalContent = getModalContent();

      if (!modalContent) {
        return Promise.resolve(0);
      }

      return patchPostcode(modalContent);
    }

    function startShortWatch() {
      if (isPolling) {
        return;
      }

      isPolling = true;

      tryUpgradeModalWidgets().then(function (count) {
        if (count > 0) {
          isPolling = false;
          return;
        }

        var startedAt = Date.now();
        var timerId = setInterval(function () {
          tryUpgradeModalWidgets().then(function (count) {
            if (count > 0) {
              clearInterval(timerId);
              isPolling = false;
              return;
            }

            if (Date.now() - startedAt >= WATCH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
              clearInterval(timerId);
              isPolling = false;
            }
          });
        }, WATCH_INTERVAL_MS);
      });
    }

    function bindTriggerButtons() {
      var buttons = document.querySelectorAll(TRIGGER_SELECTOR);

      for (var i = 0; i < buttons.length; i++) {
        if (buttons[i].getAttribute("data-zr-trigger-bound") === "true") {
          continue;
        }
        buttons[i].addEventListener("click", startShortWatch);
        buttons[i].setAttribute("data-zr-trigger-bound", "true");
      }
    }

    bindTriggerButtons();

    // If modal markup is already present, upgrade immediately without watching.
    tryUpgradeModalWidgets();
  })();
</script>
          </div>
         </div>
        </div>
       </div>
      </div>
     </div>
    </div>
   </div>
